===== PAGE 44 [page-d58a593ad327ee7b1bdb5c25] =====
--- seg-db834670cff61aac8ff64d6f [machine-unreviewed] ---
In addressing the question of whether there are extraterrestrial visitors, Luiz Carlos Menezes, a physicist and researcher at the University of São Paulo, disagrees with the behavior of what he calls these "possible and strange visitors" David: "If these UFOs carry colored lights, they are truly not trying to pass unnoticed and, if they are really ETs, they should be much farther away. So why don't they come for a cup of coffee?" Menezes does not rule out the possibility of life outside of Earth, but does not credit interstellar travel.

He explains, "Our solar system is very difficult to have life. But it's possible that there might be a desolated world at a great distance from our universe. According to the laws of physics, these visitors are not impossible, but we cannot affirm that any strange object is a visitor from another galaxy. I don't want to create any skepticism, but never again will I search for a fragment of an OVN to prove its existence."

The lack of concrete evidence, he argues, makes ufology considered a science difficult. "What there are are visual evidences, hypotheses that stimulate a fantasy," he emphasizes. In the opinion of Ernest Hamburger, also a physicist at USP, "If there were life on other planets, it is possible that these extraterrestrial beings could have developed intelligence and a technology that would allow long-duration interstellar travel." But he considers that Earth has unique characteristics, and that on another planet, there is unlikely to be a being similar to humans. "If there is life," he stresses, "it must be completely different. So different that it's impossible to imagine."

--- seg-947cc822a5fe93b33fdac31a [needs-review] ---
Converging with the physical theories of the origins of extraterrestrial beings—trailed by ufologists, such as Ernest Hamburger and Luiz Carlos Menezes—proved surprising. Menezes debates: "Is it possible that a man could be sent from another planet living in subterranean or marine habitats? What kind of being would that be? A human? A creature?" As for the other hypotheses, he simply shakes his head and defines: "Nonsense. Nonsense." Hamburger also comments: "The Earth has been so thoroughly explored that if there had been a discovery of extraterrestrial life among us, it would have been proven by now." And regarding the future travelers' opinion: "According to what we know from astrophysics, time can only move forward."

"Do you want to hear a curious story?" Menezes asks. And he relates:

"When I was 13 years old, I used to pass through the ironworks of Guaratinguetá. One night, walking down a street called Rangel Pestana, very different from what exists in São Paulo, I felt like looking up at the sky. Suddenly, I saw a UFO pass by at an incredible speed. I was left in a state of shock, and the next day, as soon as the morning light broke, I returned to the same spot. I realized that at that point, on the top of the hill, there were tall antennas. The UFOs were like beacons reflecting off the wires. It was an illusion...

"Although I have never seen or dreamed of UFOs, Ivette Carina Jabour Khalil, director of CETE-PE—Clinical Terapêutica Extraterrestre, a psychic clinic, admits the possibility of extraterrestrial life. 'But we cannot consider everything we see as a UFO,' she clarifies."

This is a detailed account of an encounter with an extraterrestrial being by Thiago Machado, a 37-year-old individual. The event occurred on February 12, 1969, in Pirassununga, Brazil.

At the time, UFO sightings were common, but this particular day marked the landing of an OVNI (Unidentified Flying Object) on a piece of land in Vila Pinheiros. Approximately 30 people gathered to witness the event. Thiago, driven by curiosity, approached the object.

The extraterrestrial entity was described as being large, with a diameter of seven meters. It had a metallic appearance, with legs and arms made of steel, wearing boots and carrying a revolver. As Thiago neared, the being emitted a strange sound, resembling a "rangy" noise.

Thiago attempted to communicate with the beings through these sounds, which seemed to be their language. He noticed that their physical features, including an eye on the forehead and different skin color, were distinct from humans.

The beings, sensing his intentions, retreated. Thiago realized they found his behavior unusual. He threw a stone at the being, which caused it to emit a jet of light, and the entity disappeared into a sticky substance within its clothing.

Thiago's thoughts were read by the beings, who understood his desire to approach them. However, they responded with a powerful beam of light, causing him to fall to the ground. He was left with injuries that required a two-year treatment to heal properly.

Today, Thiago works as a driver in São Paulo, Brazil. He is aware that his experience was extraordinary and that his encounter with the extraterrestrial being was a unique event. Despite the attention his case received from ufologists in Brazil and abroad, he is not preoccupied with whether others believe his story or not.

In the cases of more strange occurrences, the "chupa-chupas" or "space vampires," encountering these beings and what the extraterrestrial beings are, is peaceful. Ufologist and journalist Ira-mace Pires explains this phenomenon, which has been occurring since the 1980s.

They have been happening in the Amazon region, particularly in the Pará area. Trails of a light, the "chupa-chupas," as they are nicknamed by the local population, can drain up to half a liter of blood from their victims. Over 90% of the victims are women, usually fainting with a circular mark on their left side, leading researchers to conclude that the blood was extracted by a mechanical species. Near the location, strange object marks are found in the soil.
--- seg-a27d32c18386b6d6db01a6ad [machine-unreviewed] ---
Sexual Contacts
Antonio Carlos Ferreira, in the early hours
of June 28, 1979, when he was 21, was working
at Transmóveis FA-FÁ, a company in Mirassol,
São Paulo, when he encountered a strange object
in the factory patio. Three men approached him.
Before he could react, he was paralyzed by a beam
of light and taken inside a ship. After being subjected
to various examinations, he was taken to a room
with a group of women.

The woman was very different. She had pointed
ears, curly bright red hair, red pubic hair, tanned
skin, small breasts, and teeth like ours. Her face
was large with prominent lips. She was taller than
the other women. She looked at him with a strange
energy that made him feel uncomfortable.

The extraterrestrials removed his clothing, poured
a special oil over his body, and forced him into a
sexual relationship with one of the women. Antonio
Carlos was found in a state of shock and with signs
of burns and marks on his body.

After some years, the extraterrestrials returned to
visit him again, this time to show their son, born
from that relationship. A ugly child, according to
the words of the father - the child had pointed ears,
faded skin, and bright red hair, just like his mother.

--- seg-a44565eeb52aba3635a023ce [machine-unreviewed] ---
in what way or for what reason
conflicts that have been internalized. If you are angry with someone, don't accuse them of being cowardly
or envious or selfish. In the case of a normal person, who has the notion of giving and knows their limits, they understand these feelings. But in the case of a psychotic, these sentiments become actions that affect only themselves. If the psychotic imagines a UFO, it's an external fantasy that they transform into something concrete because in their mental state, the UFO is projected into the external world.

Still, according to the explanations of the psychiatrist, it's possible for an individual, even without being psychotic, to transform a perception of OVNI and UFO into a reality—a person experiencing anxiety may end up materializing a situation they themselves created. In addition to seeing the UFO, it causes physical and psychological symptoms such as skin eruptions, nervous imbalances, hallucinations, appetite loss, and other symptoms that can be mistaken for contact with OVNIs.
--- seg-70bfd6ba7225369b57c88f70 [needs-review] ---
Fantasy
The functionality of the Air Force — some of the FAB — considers "fantastical" as information from ufologists who claim there is an exchange secret between Brazil and the United States to investigate UFO sightings.

They deny the existence of this agreement, and these same officials also deny that the Air Force maintains a secret file on UFOs. According to a general officer of the Air Force, the ministry does not maintain any organ or even a section aimed at specifically analyzing UFO cases. "Each case," guaranteed this same official, "is analyzed based on the information received, as occurred recently, when the ministry convened a press conference with the presence of journalists from abroad, to clarify the facts."

Another point of the ufologists' affirmations that the Air Force officials considered "fantastical" is their claim that officials are required to make statements about UFO sightings. For these officials, this is not true, as the Air Force has recently been investigating UFO sightings in Sao Paulo and Brasilia through radar control systems.

BRASÍLIA
A NATION STATE
The sky of São Paulo, São José dos Campos and Rio de Janeiro was virtually invaded last night by more than 20 unidentified flying objects, causing a state of general alert at air defense bases in Brazil and the mobilization of four supersonic planes—two Mirage and two F-5. The president of the Republic, José Sarney, was personally alerted to the incident.

Official information was transmitted yesterday at the Planalto Palace by the Minister of Aeronautics, Brig. Octávio Moreira Lima, who expects today to receive reports from the Air Force High Command, the Integrated Air Defense and Air Traffic Control Center (Cindacta), and the pilots who participated in the interception. Brazilian air defenses were activated within minutes, and the radar screens became completely cluttered with the unidentified objects.

The planes took off simultaneously from the bases in Anápolis, 150 kilometers from Brasília, and Santa Cruz, in Rio, with explicit orders to make contact and identify the mysterious objects. According to the minister, the pilots reported seeing luminous objects of intense light, reflecting various colors. At one point, a supersonic plane, one of the P-6 interceptors, was surprised while being followed by six objects on one side and seven on the other. The minister admits that the information from the pilots is "fantastic" and, at this moment, there is no explanation for it; the official office, he says, is dealing with "an inexplicable phenomenon."

--- seg-fae26602c1397326c378f3c0 [needs-review] ---
In an unprecedented event in Brazilian history, the head of the Brazilian Air Force, Brigadier General Octavio Moreira Lima, announced yesterday that four supersonic jets from the FAB attempted to intercept, on the last night of the second week of May, "unidentified flying objects" (UFOs) flying over São Paulo, Rio de Janeiro, and São José dos Campos.

Before making his official statement to journalists, General Moreira Lima revealed to President José Sarney that at 8 PM on that night, upon detecting the UFOs in his radar, the Air Defense Center and Air Traffic Control (CINDACTA) activated their general alarm. Five F-5 jets took off from Anápolis and Santa Cruz Air Bases, with the mission to identify and intercept the objects.

According to the general, one of the F-5s was surrounded by thirteen "luminous points" from the UFOs. Seven of these points descended to a low altitude, but the pilot was unable to identify the UFOs.
--- seg-51336f33efffcc8be4e40601 [needs-review] ---
Following the president, the radiomen reported only "defence objects" - metallic objects, solid surfaces and cloud formations. This final hypothesis has already been discarded by Moreira Lima: "In the three cities, the sky was absolutely clear," he says. Today, the minister will receive reports from the four pilots who were on duty on Monday. After analyzing the documents, he will send them to the Major Aerodome of the Air Force, which will conduct more in-depth studies. "We have nothing to hide. Everything will be disclosed to the press," he promised.

President Sarney, following the report of Moreira Lima, did not show concern. "I would act as any curious person would," the minister said, who had already spoken with the president about this subject during the third day of the week, during a meeting with the president of El Salvador in Itamarati.

This was the first time in Brazil that a ministerial figure officially acknowledged the identification of UFOs.

Issues of this nature, generally controversial, are kept under a veil of secrecy, and when they reach public knowledge, they are often distorted.
--- seg-71f410a6c1effb4df364daf3 [needs-review] ---

Yesterday,
Moreira
Lima
not only confirmed a perception of UFOs, but also acknowledged that there are records in his ministry of events similar to those: "The appearance of unidentified flying objects has already occurred in Brazil, but never with such intensity."

"We have pilots ready to take off within five minutes, should hostile aircraft be identified in our airspace," he said, exemplifying: "In PJ82, during the Malvinas War, two 'Mirage' fighter jets forced a Cuban aircraft to land in Brasília."

This time, however, the situation was quite different: the fighters were activated, they did everything they were supposed to do, but the pilots ended up becoming... a mess.

Pilots confirm
having seen OVNIs
Two
military pilots, in a collective interview yesterday, confirmed having really seen non-identified luminous objects in the sky not far from São Paulo and Brasília.

In addition to these luminous objects, strange echoes in the radars impressed the Ministry of Aeronautics, which did not name a commission to investigate the case.

Alerted by the Air Control Center of São José dos Campos (SP), the Embraer pilot Alcyr Pereira da Silva, who lives in Brasília, was the first to see the "point" luminous, in the direction of the city of São Paulo. He tried to get closer to it as soon as it appeared.

The lieutenant of the Brazilian Air Force (FAB), Kleber Caldas Marinho, piloting an F-5 that took off from the airbase of Santa Cruz (RJ), also reported having seen a "point of light of a different color," which moved in a horizontal direction. He affirmed that at the same time, the same point changed its color to green and then to red.

Kleber tried to get closer to 24 kilometers from the point, which was moving towards the sea, when the pilot returned to the base.

Captain Márcio Brasil de Jordão, who was piloting another F-5, took off from the same base 15 minutes later and saw a "fixed point of a different color that did not move."

"With the repercussions of the unidentified flying objects that appeared in the skies of São Paulo on the night of May 19, the obvious need for clarifications arose. The first suspicion, of course, fell on space artifacts. Satellites and space shuttles orbiting below 400 km can not only be observed on radar screens, but can also be seen with the naked eye when illuminated by the sun against a dark sky. Thus, it was suspected that a space body, possibly a carrier rocket that had lost its necessary speed to counter Earth's gravity, was reentering the atmosphere and subsequently disintegrating."

On Monday, 19/05, the ionized layer that covers the Earth in a sphere shape broke through, causing the telemetry signals from the Soviet orbital station 'Salyut-7' at frequencies of 19953 and 19954 Khz to reach São Paulo with extremely high intensity, due to the lack of attenuation by the ionized layer, and at the same time, terrestrial communications between radio amateurs in bands of 10, 15 and 20 meters were interrupted at distances that depend on reflection by the ionospheric layer. What is ionization? By definition, ionization is the process of removing electrons from atoms or molecules, creating ions.
--- seg-bc80b269684d35a19facebbc [needs-review] ---
Ionization is the unfolding of molecules into two or more electrically charged atoms, for example, in the ionosphere, by collision caused by high energy bombardments. When electrons are mixed with positive ions in approximately equal numbers, they form a highly conductive plasma capable of reflecting even decimetric waves as if they were metallic objects. These plasma volumes can be detected on radar screens.

As for the possibility of rapid displacement of ionization of the mass, I can give an example of a personal experiment, occurring during the already mentioned solar maximum 21, in V H F, in the 50 MHz range where the phenomenon can be most observed. Using the lowest power, I spoke as if locally, on November 20, 1980, at 0000 UTC with the EL2FY station from Monrovia, Africa, at 0005 UTC with the VS6BE station from Hong Kong, at 0019 UTC with the LU9AEA station from Argentina and at 0023 UTC with the CE3DZ station from Chile, all in less than 25 minutes. The following night, at 2347 UTC with the LU9MA station from Argentina, at 2358 UTC with the VP2VGR station from the Leeward & Windward Islands in the Caribbean, at 0002 UTC with the PJ2DEW station from Curaçao and at 0020 UTC with the WH6ADA station from Hawaii, all in less than 35 minutes. When the ionization moved, it opened up propagation to one area and closed it to all others.

Simultaneously with the reflection of radio-electromagnetic waves, ionized masses can emit light through the known effect called the aurora effect, which may have given pilots, on the clear night of Monday, 19/05, the impression of true objects.

To conclude, some clarifications about Bramsat. It is an advanced association of Brazilian radio amateurs, with its headquarters in São Paulo, chaired by radio amateur PY2BJO, Engineer Júnior Torres de Castro. It enjoys high prestige among scientists linked to NASA, for having provided significant services to the space community, as was evident during the visit to São Paulo of the scientist from Kasa and former president of Amsat, Thomas A. Clark, radio amateur W3IWI, who, in addition to being a PhD, is one of the world's leading authorities in radio astronomy.

It is interesting to mention that Bramsat, which seeks to stay at the forefront of space technology through its relations with NASA and Amsat, and which maintains a low-noise ground station 30 km from São Paulo, also provides free space assistance to the national industry of satellite receivers, having already collaborated with Zirok during the development of the parabolic antenna and the

between the days 12th of March and

they observed "three alien human
interplanetary visitors" operating
"very advanced equipment for
formation and severe and replete
with vigils" (windows), as Scortecci
says in his article. The contacts
were reported during nearly a week,
with communications between the
Soviets and the "human
interplanetarians."
' To the surprise of the astronauts,
the beings approached approximately
30 meters from the Salyut 6 ship.

Then they translated the format of the interplanetary communication and exchange device into English, approximately 9-10 meters in diameter, with 16 transparent areas, illuminated, semilucent, and eight in the center, others in a radial line, suggesting some connection with the motor system of the parallel.

By its brilliancy and appearance, the cosmonauts concluded that the material of the vessel was metallic. They did not perceive any reentry or unusualness. Inscriptions, marks or continuous lines on the surface of the sphere, perfectly polished, but internally, the Russians observed that everything was well lit, showing a conventional control cabin with panels of controls, commands, and monochromatic coatings.

--- seg-0e779492688883c4b3d5728a [needs-review] ---
BRASÍLIA — 0 President José Sarney was informed on Monday night by the Integrated Center of Air Defense and Air Traffic Control (CINDACTA) about the passage of strange objects in the Brazilian sky. As Supreme Commander of the Armed Forces, Mr. Sarney decided to identify three flying objects located near São José dos Campos, which were later destroyed by F-SE and Mirage III fighter jets from Santa Cruz Air Bases in Anápolis. The decision was not taken. The unidentified objects fled in the direction of the coastal plain, accompanied by an F-5E, which interrupted pursuit after reaching the 200-mile limit from the mainland. Indignant about the matter, President Sarney stated that these OVNs (Unidentified Flying Objects) had caused him much concern:

"This seems like Antonio Carlos (Magalhães)" — he commented, ironically, as Minister of Aeronautics, Octavio Júlio Moreira Lima.
--- seg-73289ed15d46a98411314963 [needs-review] ---
The first unidentified objects were the new president of Petrobras, Ozires Silva. His Xingu aircraft was performing final landing procedures in Sao Jose dos Campos when lights were perceived that might interfere with the aerial traffic of the region.

The Xingu pilot reported the incident to the Sao Jose dos Campos tower, which located a small object and activated the defense center in Brasilia. The defense center located three objects by radar at a distance of about four miles, and saw three lights, in green, red, and white, retreating in the direction of the sea.

The on-board instruments experienced interference until 22:15h, when the pursuit was interrupted due to a lack of fuel. At this moment, other unidentified radar contacts were verified in the vicinity of Anapolis. Three Mirage III aircraft, armed with Sidewinder and Matra 530 missiles, took off to intercept the object.

===== PAGE 67 [page-592c7647b7173b6bb0ebf8b2] =====
--- seg-99c232f00b1f4131b8ad1b5e [needs-review] ---
• UFOs

A question, where do flying saucers come from? Sir: "The event of last day 19, when Air Force planes pursued for hours a fleet of 21 flying saucers, reignited, in a spectacular way, interest in ufology, since officially - through the Minister of Aeronautics - the phenomenon was recognized in the mentioned case. However, although the ungewöhnliness of the event, old ufo-logical questions were revived that continue to intrigue everyone. They are: Where do the saucers come from? Why do they come? And how do they come? ... These questions are the target of debates, hypotheses, divagations, and conjectures that have not yet led to anything concrete about the subject. It is known that the phenomenon is real; that UFOs sometimes become invisible, that there are translucent UFOs, that others change visibly in organic form when in our atmosphere, and that their shapes - discs, cigars, balls, hats, and even humanoids - differ in various studied cases." Despite using the most modern methods and equipment in its research, ufology is still far from an effective answer regarding the origin of UFOs and their respective passengers. The urological casuistry is rich in evidence of first, second, and third-degree contacts, where at least one can venture the hypothesis of numerous origins. According to this studied casuistry, UFOs have technological variations between them that indicate inferior and superior intelligences. The humanoids would vary in height from about 60 cm to approximately three meters. The logic of the universe leads us to the existence of several worlds. However, it is said that if UFOs come from another solar system other than our own, even traveling at the speed of light — 300,000 km/s — it would still take them almost a hundred years to undertake a journey of this order. It is at the root
of this problem that a conjecture is made
at the forefront of ufology, due to its own
invisibilization, and sometimes, organic changes
of UFOs, into parallel spaces — UFOs would move through
these spaces at a fantastic speed, thus covering enormous
distances in seconds.
- Thus, if this is true, it is that here, at least theoretically,
is the explanation to two of those questions. To wit; where
do they come from, how do they come? According to this logic of
the outer space, without abandoning the possibility of an
intraterrestrial phenomenon (there
--- seg-831b692963f8abb05ac75d30 [needs-review] ---
Gerson Mjciel witnessed the *UFOs
a theory according to which the Earth would
be hollow and inhabited by an advanced civilization). However, if from outer space, it can only be through these parallel spaces or, as some suggest, by antigravitational and anti-inertia systems, or even possibly by time travelers, or fantastically, still by beings from the astral world. But if from outer space or even intraterrestrial, or if from two or more concurrent hypotheses, why? In all the studied casuistics, it is not possible, not even subjectively, to conjecture a solution to this question, undoubtedly the most important and comprehensive ufological inquiry. Thus, there are cases in which the UFOs reveal bellicosity; they abduct and even cause physical damage and deaths. Others, in a highly superior number (like the case of September 19), are peaceful. These ignore our bellicosity (terrestrial), maintaining a distance and not reacting when attacked. Others, also in great number, maintain friendly contacts with terrestrial beings, warning about nuclear danger (would there be a connection with the recent nuclear accident at the Soviet plant or even with the Angra dos Reis plants?), and many other dangers that can lead to the destruction of the planet.

However, the diversity of the phenomenon is great, both in the technology of the devices as well as in the form of the crew, or even as regards their intentions when visiting us. The problem is complex. The origins may be multiple. Thus, man finds himself facing his greatest challenge, which is to unravel one mystery that will certainly open the doors to the secrets of the cosmos, thus taking flight on his most fantastic adventure, that is, to participate objectively in the Universal Brotherhood, as Christ said: "In my Father's house there are many abodes." (Hélio Carvilho from "Notícias de Brasília", C t p i f I,

UFOs also choose the Xingu—

The sky of Xingu is clear, full of stars. In the vast open space, a bright light appeared in 1976, forcing people to leave their homes at night to investigate. When they arrived at the location, they saw a strong, rapid light. It was an unforgettable experience for them, and a strong, brilliant light was involved.

The sound of Xingu is the nature of small creatures living in the rivers' banks, the winds rustling the leaves of the trees. It's a perfect history of love. The perfect contact between a human and a UFO.

But some people have seen various appearances. It seems that everyone has had this kind of experience.

One person recounts: "I saw a UFO passing through the sky, like a bright star, over the Xingu river. It was a large, bright light." But for many, these sightings remain a mystery.

It seems unlikely that some secret services or governments would deliberately conceal such encounters, as the potential implications for humanity would be immense. If extraterrestrial life exists, it is reasonable to assume that they would have sought a peaceful and collaborative relationship with humans, rather than engaging in a "war" or "boarding" of our planet.

3.1. — Recapitulation of previous matter
--- seg-4d9861645c4c50f1309c77e6 [needs-review] ---
Let's recall with a few moments from Parts I and II, clearly stating that, in a certain period, the development of technology on Earth was so rapid that in 6 to 8 minutes it could be reduced to naught by another realm.

From this, it is evident that the existence of our civilization—which guarantees our sustenance—is threatened by external intervention.

It is not a question of whether we should retaliate or not, but rather, given the circumstances, we must take immediate action to defend our planet.

The responsibility for this lies primarily with our leaders, the majority of our race, who belong to nations that claim to be Christian, but in practice, act otherwise.

In this ultimate analysis, the warnings and extraterrestrial claims made by such figures as Joseph Allen Hynek, Fred Whipple, and others during this final era of our planet's history, when the course of technological advancement between Earth and the rest of the universe has become increasingly intertwined, should not be dismissed as non-existent.

In 1957, at the launch of the Russian satellite Sputnik, Hynek, who had 75 years of experience in the field, stated that "the time is at hand when we must expect the appearance of extraterrestrial intelligence."

This bulletin, intended for the scientific community, also aims to draw the attention of governments to the necessity of studying these phenomena with an open mind. The opportunity to understand the reality of extraterrestrial life should not be ignored.

The same magazine, in its intent to inform the public, presents evidence and testimonies regarding UFO sightings, not as proof of non-existent extraterrestrial origins, but as a call for serious investigation.

4.2. - The uproar of the night of May 19, 1986
--- seg-c70747d3ffdff11c0a4973d0 [needs-review] ---
To the rest of the global territory -
news about the sight of UFOs in Brazil are reaching in the form of "UFOs were guides in Brazil" (3), with the subtitle "Enigmatic aerial objects keep FAB in suspense...". One of the national newspapers (4) (Diário da Tarde - Belo Horizonte, 22/5/86 - "The observer from the horizon, from the air, saw 3 UFOs") expresses with its following words the inescapable reality of the overflights:

"São José dos Campos - Some hours later (the following day, 19/5/86), after receiving a message from the President of the Republic, the mission of checking the interests of Petrobras in the land and at sea (at the port, 21 hours) was carried out by Colonel Ozires Silva, who was again assigned a mission that was almost impossible, with a rapid and acute curiosity: being a pilot of a Xing (prefix PT-MBZ), he pursued, during 30 minutes, three unidentified flying objects (0 VNI); he was unable to catch them up, because they changed their position rapidly (•-••) (*).

The co-pilot of the aircraft, Alcir Pereira da Silva, who works at Embraer, was in contact with the local airport control tower when the decision to use the jet was made. It was decided that the level of use would be limited to 1,000 meters altitude, because it was considered that at that level the three unidentified objects (UIOs) would be more visible.

The three UIOs appeared in the form of three bright lights, moving in a straight line at a constant speed. They were not aircraft, because they did not have wings or any kind of propulsion system visible. They did not resemble any known natural phenomenon.

The three UIOs maintained their formation, moving in perfect synchronization, at a distance of approximately 500 meters from each other. They did not show any kind of communication or interaction with the jet. They simply moved in a straight line, disappearing from view after a few minutes.

The three UIOs appeared and disappeared in less than 10 minutes, leaving the jet pilots with many questions. What were they? Where did they come from? Where did they go? Why did they not interact with the jet? The answers to these questions remain unknown, but the experience left a lasting impression on those who had the chance to witness it."
--- seg-829b7a95a299a71aa8edc77d [not-required] ---
(*)
--- seg-85e56a0da184727d4926071b [not-required] ---

On the same day, 22/5/86
(13) a UFO was seen over the city of Maringá, state of Paraná. This object was filmed from two points: once from the proximity of the Municipal Park of Exposition, and another time, from the broadcast tower of TV Globo. The filmed object and then
--- seg-2ca1d6fcaaff5a05c2f7e3e1 [needs-review] ---
Displayed on TV Globo (in the "Fan-tastic" segment) and broadcast at a later time, it was fascinating to watch. ===== PAGE 106 [page-d18af553830372bfd417f3ee] =====
--- seg-f91e722162646c88e6608791 [needs-review] ---
from antecedent event . In the municipality of Liberated, in the state of Minas Gerais, a group of children was observing "luminous objects descending and stopping without ceasing" . Also, in the South, in the state of Santa Catarina, on the same fifth day of the month, at night, the OVNIs were seen in Blumenau (SC). They were described as "point-like luminosities" that would appear and disappear in 5 minutes.

(14)
In addition, several people in the city of Sete and those gathered at the "Biulanche" intersection observed the objects, as for example, the students Marcelo Clemente and Marco Babitonga.

However, for the following day, on the sixth, at 7:30 PM, the object was again seen in the mineral region of Serra do Cipo, on the road to Jose Pedro, 30 years old, a resident of Tabuleiro. It was then that, in the crossroad previously mentioned, it was confronted by a "large negotiation", a specific type of bacias with green lights blinking. It seemed to fluctuate a little, taking over the entire street.

Jose turned around and returned to his people. Shortly after, at 22 hours, the same object, or another identical one, was seen by several residents of Tabuleiro, causing them to change their direction.

One of them, Jose Ferreira, 55 years old (father of 7 children), reported that "well positioned in the group, a light appeared, blinking, when everyone fled running."

According to information from other witnesses, the object had the size of a Fusca car; it was round and had a specific "jewel" part.

In the early evening of May 27, 1986, in the rural area of Boa Pastor, 300 meters from the town of Bandeirante (PR), 18 people from the local community witnessed unusual phenomena. For approximately an hour, they observed strange lights, which would circle the pastureland, creating 100-meter radius circles, between two mangueiras (mango trees).

Jose Geraldo, an assistant, reported that a specific type of light from the object caused some parts of the vehicle's panel to function again, suddenly restoring their functionality.

That same night, radio journalist Osvaldo Luis Gonçalves from Radio Cabiuna in Bandeirante interviewed the witness, Cleide Silva, along with a neighbor, Jose Lato. They both described seeing luminous balls that would rotate and move, creating circles on the pasture.

In the night, after the end of the football match broadcast on television, the farmer Paulo Pereira Campos (nicknamed "Nego"), aged 30, with his three children, was in his field with a companion. They were heading to their residences, leaving the factory of Bateia, located 5 kilometers from the center.

At a distance of 300 meters from the street, in a pasture, they observed a bright light in the sky. They stopped to watch it, a strange phenomenon. To observe the object for a moment, the companion of Nego lit a cigarette.

Immediately, the light rose and moved in the direction of the group. It was a bright ball, with a glowing tail, that kept getting brighter. The ball followed the farmer and his friend, who were now in a hurry. The object seemed to be attracted to them.

Nego informed them that they were trying to escape, "but we can't make it." He thought the ball was following them for their direction.

The object, which had been over the bamboo forest, suddenly moved quickly to the top of a nearby hill, 800 meters away, where it disappeared.

The two children ran towards home, while the farmer, still startled, followed them, his son in his arms. They continued to observe the phenomenon (which at that moment was a sleeping one). Nego observed shadows passing "prala e praca" (a local expression) in front of the object.

After some time, the light, which had been fragmented and unclear, became intense. It was when the farmer heard a cry and rushed to the top of the hill, where he found his son, exhausted, at the foot of the fence.

MOACIR BAIANO CASE, IN PATI DO ALFERES (RJ).
--- seg-6c5a1df40ef70f503c926d82 [needs-review] ---
5.1. - Episode Summary and Data
--- seg-28703e6f97f80a0fa3b5b4c3 [needs-review] ---
5.1.1. — Summary
While directing a friend's funeral at 7 p.m., a witness is approached by a giant, approximately 3 meters tall, who invites them for a conversation. Without leaving the funeral, the witness is accompanied by the stranger in a nearby field. Upon opening up, the witness reveals a recent discovery.

In this location, they encounter a terrisque (a discovered creature). Initially, through force and then by observation, the witness inspects the creature, always accompanied by its transport. It is noted that the creature does not have a visible means of transportation, and it is seen traversing a known and open area.

On the following day, during the morning, the witness suddenly encounters the ufonaut (a creature from outer space) in front of them, a new development from the previous day's events. From this point, the witness is sent in a direct path from the funeral to the creature's location. And in the midst of it all, the witness's spouse does not recognize the creature or the transport.

The witness then describes a fierce, mouth-to-mouth struggle between the ufonaut and the creature, which ends with the ufonaut disappearing again, all of which occurred in silence and without being noticed.

Comments on the witness's testimony regarding this and other episodes in their life are made, with the details appearing to be factual.
--- seg-f290852cc8e8f2b2da681281 [machine-unreviewed] ---
it is to be related to some of its enigmatic facets.
--- seg-f6a0e80af9791080a898d638 [needs-review] ---
5.1.2. - Data
Testimony name:
Moacir
Elia de França, commonly known as Moa-
cir Baiano, born
on
28-I-32, in João Pessoa, capital of the state of Paraíba.
Date of the episode: 3rd of October, 1977 (end of the episode in December).
Location of the episode: Rua Belevedere, #245 (formerly the Hotel Belevedere), at the top of a 30-meter cliff, situated in the Goiabal valley, near 1500 meters from the center of the small town of Paty do Alferes, south of the state of Rio de Janeiro. Currently, the witness resides at Rua B, #345, in the neighborhood of Arcozelo, in the same city.

Time of the investigations:
Started in 1977 (we do not know the exact date) and continued until 1986, in the months of:
- March (days 11, 14, and 20)
- May (days 18)
- June (days 26 and 16 of August)

5.2. - Introduction
--- seg-782576119ebe62c6e371c37e [needs-review] ---
In 1977, we were approached by Carlos Arthur Ribeiro da Rocha (Carlinhos Sideral), who presented us with Waldir Vieira (now deceased), who had been involved in the case in his well-known radio program on Radio Globo.

Immediately, we began in 1977 to look for Moacir Baiano in a certain location, unfortunately not indicated by Waldir. However, in this case, as Moacir was absent from home for professional reasons (painting walls), we did not have the opportunity to meet him, although we left a message for him via postal service.

Recently, we were reminded of the name and case of this person by someone who knew Moacir personally. In this encounter, this person kindly helped us to locate Moacir's new residence, as it had been very difficult to find him.

Moacir is a person of the "truculento" type, a native of the Brazilian "hinterland" of the North, a mix of our "quatro rasas" (four races) - indigenous, African, European, and Portuguese. He told us right away, to characterize the duration of his life, that "work has taken him from night to day," working together with ten to fifteen mouths (children) to sustain his family.
--- seg-65b6e8c1bc75a9acda75f257 [needs-review] ---
Really, before reporting a case, we must confess that Moacir, for his teachings, has been a beacon to the rest of the people, although he has studied a professional career in Paraiba and, in 1955, a significant event occurred in Rio de Janeiro. He returned an artistic afternoon in the country of his origin, receiving various distinctions and honors in his city and in Rio de Janeiro.

But let us draw attention to other qualities of his, which are of a psychological nature, such as his ability to locate defects and correct them in appearances whose basic functioning eludes him. For example, when, on a certain day, a radio device was brought to him for repair, he proved to be a competent and knowledgeable technician.

However, curious, Moacir first turned on the current electrical radio, successfully convening himself of the defect. Entangled in a sudden urgency, he seized the device and, in a rush, threw it into the fire, causing the radio to be lost in the ashes. *

How was not his surprise when, instead of finding the device shattered, he discovered it still intact and functioning.

The other relatives of Moacir and the more prominent qualities of his character will be made known later.*

5.5 - The Cape Fear Episode
--- seg-10ac044657a63912e054783b [needs-review] ---
In the month of December, in 1971, and the 15 years and 6 years prior to the UFO logical episode of Pat do Alferes - according to his relative, Moacir - who witnessed the event, we will not venture to classify.

The case in which this event occurred was a passage with a peculiar phenomenon, near Cabo Frio, a well-known coastal area of Pat do K_feres 25D km approximately. The next step was organized by a senior officer, Jair, our protagonist, Moacir.

The excursion, by bus, was to begin in the early evening, taking about 30 people from Pat do Alferes.
--- seg-4f3cf220671675b4170cefee [needs-review] ---
In May - June of some of the participants, currently living. Among these, Mr. G M (killed by the "Jilo" gang), representative of the baby food and refrigeration brand, whom we visited at his site on Estrada RJ-125, NS64 055. Another participant was Mrs. Nilza Silva Melo, resident at Rua Vicente Freitas, no. 90, in Paty do Alferes. These two testified that there had been a 4-hour power outage during the night, as Moacir, one of their organizers, had reported 12 hours earlier, and had been repaired by 6 p.m.

Moacir informed us that they had not been robbed on the previous night, despite having been with their others, on the beach.

===== PAGE 125 [page-0a4fb85e4e86616177d06902] =====
--- seg-d198a56d923e2fdc70a50db4 [needs-review] ---
It is, regretfully and suddenly, transported to a peculiar landscape of Cabo Frio, Brazil, where there was a peculiar sight of a peculiar sight of a peculiar landscape with no visible vegetation, only trees and plants along the shores (Letter film 238M). The sun, usually so vibrant, was absent, and the sky was overcast (Center film 239M). Despite the hot atmosphere, Moacir, gasping for air, felt a strange lack of breath, as if he were in a different realm.

After walking a considerable distance, Moacir noticed a peculiar structure, a line of about 100 small houses, constructed closely together, resembling nothing he had ever seen before, not even in the orange-red desert of the world he knew (Figure n.30). They had clear, small windows, and their design seemed to be a mix of different styles, perhaps from various indigenous cultures (Letter films 169M, 225M, 171M, 242M, and 178M).

The structures were bathed in a soft, mysterious light, with clear tones of green, blue, and brown (Letter films 169M, 225M, 171M, 242M, and 178M). Moacir, fascinated, approached the houses, hoping to interact with their inhabitants, but after 10 minutes of careful maneuvering, he found himself still 50 meters away, unable to get any closer.

5.6 — Other Episodes
--- seg-f08006980ad8f3357f82bbdd [needs-review] ---
After an experience living in Paty do Alferece, Moacir became interested in ufology. Also participating in a rumorous popular procession that heads to the district of Casimir de Abreu, in the State of Rio de Janeiro, at the beginning of 1980, this commitment led to the presence of Dr. Linda Guillion from Paty do Alferece.

Consulting the SBEDV report (I 32/135, page 30), it is noted that the daily "Fluminense" (3/30/80) reported that Rogerio de Lima and his group also made UFO sightings in Casimir de Abreu. In addition, the SBEDV received a photograph that had been torn, depicting an event in Casimir de Abreu, by a ufological group.

In that same year, in the 1982 video, Moacir was in his car, driving from Vassouras to Paty do Alferece, with 15 other people: his daughter Monj, two men, and two women, Noemia Rosa and Leila. The car had already passed the location of Sagrada Familia and was heading towards Morro Azul when it encountered a group of 50 meters of people walking in the direction of the professional educator Rodolfo Fuchs (Christ Redeemer Organization). At the top of the hill, 100 meters away, the car stopped and the witnesses observed the event for 100 meters with their naked eye.

9/5 - Related Comments on the Pati of the Lieutenant Episode
--- seg-669913f5e8b4ca096bd6a881 [needs-review] ---
Once it was impossible for him to clarify the identity of the UFO (SBEDV report no. 16/12, pages 8 and 9), his wife, Mrs. Moacir, did not see the UFO, which could have been a co-witness.

Considering the more frequent occurrences of UFO sightings and the fact that Moacir's wife did not see the UFO, but only felt its proximity, we recall the story of Joaquim Murtinho, as told by CICOANI, about the case of a neighbor who witnessed an unexpected approach of an UFO from a nearby house, the widow's residence.

The student, Paulo Coutinho, was intrigued by these extraneous disturbances. Paulo was disappointed by his isolation within the aviation community. The sudden appearance of UFOs in his region, which he had never experienced before, stirred his curiosity.

In this context, Paulo noticed the absence of similar reports in the local press, which led him to extrapolate the possibility of a psychological phenomenon, a collective delusion, or perhaps a new form of technological interference.
